- The reviewer's R2 disposition decision (option (a) retroactive split of 05.1 vs option (b) free-standing post-05.1 sub-phase) was settled at the 05.1 state-6 commit in favor of option (b); 05.4 is the chosen sibling sub-phase. Future-reviewers reading STATE.md should understand that 05.1 is structurally closed at the preamble landing; 05.4 is a SIBLING under parent-05, not a child of 05.1; and the execution order ran 05.1 → 05.4 → 05.2 → 05.3, with 05.3 the closing sub-phase that flips parent-05 to `done`.

### Phase-11 state-1 brainstorm

Brainstorm session decisions (THIS commit), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") and `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork"). This is the **third post-MVP-trunk feature-family brainstorm session** (after phase-09 state-1 brainstorm `3025594` + phase-10 state-1 brainstorm `c73f44f`; the latter is the freshest shape precedent). Mirrors the phase-10 brainstorm shape. The pick was made directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation` — no two-option fork; the alternatives + 5-dimension scoring are documented below per the established brainstorm-subsection cadence.

- **Family pick: HTTP filters family (continuation; third concrete row beneath the family heading).** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Applying the same 5-dimension scoring framework used at phases 09 + 10:

  - **HTTP filters family (WINNER)** — (a) carryforward closure value **HIGH** — phase 11 is the **named close site for 09 REVIEW M2** (the H2 HCM filter-synth header-decoration gap, implementation arm — the single most-named open carryforward; the phase-10 D5 ADR-0033 Consequences amendment at `DECISIONS.md:699` named "next HTTP-filter-family phase exercising filters bilaterally on H2" as the close site verbatim). This is the decisive dimension. (b) foundation pressure **LOW** — abort reuses `Decision::StopAndSend` (07.1) + the H1 `decorate_filter_synth_response` helper (09) as the structural template for the new H2 analogue + the 04.2 `HeaderMatcher` for the gate; the only new shared type is `FractionalPercent` (envoy-config, hand-authored). (c) arch risk **LOW-MEDIUM** — abort is a twice-validated pattern (09 + 10); the new surface is the H2 `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` helper (~50-70 LoC + 2 tests, bounded + precedented by the H1 helper). (d) contract maturity **HIGH** — StopAndSend + decoration + per-filter counter wiring established at 09/10. (e) scope tractability **HIGH-MEDIUM** — ~1350-1500 LoC at-or-under the §6.1 gate.
  - **Load balancing family** — (a) carryforward closure **ZERO** (the LB seam doesn't engage the H2-decoration gap or any open carryforward); (b) zero foundation pressure; (c) LOW arch risk (extends the 02.x ClusterLoadBalancer seam; pure endpoint-pick); (d) MEDIUM contract maturity (no specific LB stats in BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT yet); (e) HIGH scope tractability (`least_request` ~600-800 LoC). **Strong second-place alternative** — rejected on the decisive carryforward-closure dimension (it leaves the most-named open carryforward, 09 M2, unclosed) + the lower contract maturity. The phase-10 brainstorm reached the identical disposition.
  - **Upstream robustness family** — (a) carryforward closure MEDIUM (closes 06.3 REVIEW I2 synthetic-5xx-backend, the named-owner family); (b) zero foundation pressure; (c) MEDIUM arch risk (active-health-check task subsystem is a new spawning primitive); (d) MEDIUM contract maturity; (e) MEDIUM scope (~1200-1400 LoC at the upper band). Rejected on the arch-risk + scope dimensions (the new task-spawning primitive is higher risk than fault's reuse-only abort path) + it does not close the higher-value 09 M2 H2 gap.
  - **Network filters family** — HIGH arch risk (new network-layer filter-chain primitive distinct from the HTTP filter chain) + LOW contract maturity (no network-filter contract entries). Rejected per the phase-09 + phase-10 identical reasoning.
  - **HTTP/3 + QUIC family** — VERY HIGH foundation pressure (`quinn` pull) + VERY HIGH arch risk (new UDP listener path; h3spec gate). Rejected per the phase-09 + phase-10 identical reasoning.
  - **gRPC family** — `tonic` permitted but no concrete gRPC backend engaged; unmotivated as the next standalone phase. Rejected per the phase-09 + phase-10 identical reasoning.
  - **xDS / dynamic config family** — HIGHEST architectural risk (entire new state machine). Rejected per the phase-09 + phase-10 identical reasoning.
  - **Observability family** — second-tier alternative; same posture as phases 09 + 10.
  - **Runtime + hot restart family** — hot restart heavy; rejected. (Note: phase-11 fault's runtime-keyed percentage overrides defer specifically because the runtime layer is unimplemented — see SPEC §4.)
  - **WASM host family** — multi-phase sub-project per §9; rejected.
  - **Deprecated / edge features** — meta-bookkeeping only; not a substantive feature family.

  Top-3 viable: **HTTP filters > Load balancing > Upstream robustness.** HTTP filters wins decisively on carryforward-closure value (it is the named close site for the most-named open carryforward, 09 REVIEW M2's implementation arm) + smallest tractable scope-without-foundations + most mature contract surface. The phase-11 SPEC §1 + §3 record the picked family + filter scope.

- **Filter pick: `envoy.filters.http.fault` (abort path; header-gated; deterministic percentage).** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. The decisive sub-consideration: **closing 09 REVIEW M2 (the H2 HCM decoration implementation arm) requires a phase that exercises a `StopAndSend`-capable HTTP filter on an H2 listener.** Among the StopAndSend-capable candidate filters (the natural vehicles for the H2-decoration close), fault (abort) is the lowest-foundation + most-tractable NEW one. Per the §9 HTTP-filter sub-feature enumeration (header_mutation DONE at 07.2; local_ratelimit DONE at 09; rbac DONE at 10), considered the remaining ~14 candidates along (StopAndSend-capable? / foundations needed / framework extension needed / differential-testable):

  - **fault — abort path (WINNER)** — StopAndSend-capable (abort short-circuits via 503); ZERO foundations (reuses StopAndSend + the H1 helper template + 04.2 HeaderMatcher; `FractionalPercent` is a small hand-authored envoy-config type); the ONLY genuinely new surface is the bounded H2 `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` helper (the 09 M2 close); the deterministic 0%/100% abort path is fully differential-testable; ~1350-1500 LoC under the §6.1 gate. The documented phase-10 runner-up. Scoped to abort-only; **fractional percentage + delay + response_rate_limit + grpc_status + downstream-controlled faults + max_active_faults defer per SPEC §4** — notably fractional percentage defers because a per-request probabilistic abort decision is **non-differential-testable** (the differential contract §7.2 does not compare probabilistic per-request outcomes across two independent proxies), and delay defers because timing is not compared by default per §7.2.
  - **jwt_authn (reject → 401)** — StopAndSend-capable + would close 09 M2 if exercised on H2; but needs a JOSE/`jsonwebtoken` foundations grant + JWKS-fetch framework. Rejected on foundation-pressure.
  - **cors (preflight → 200/204)** — StopAndSend-capable for OPTIONS preflight; but CORS is primary per-route in upstream Envoy → needs the per-route framework extension (a ~200-300 LoC gating primitive). Rejected on arch-risk; CORS remains the natural per-route-config close site per the family-wide deferral.
  - **csrf (reject → 403)** — per-route primary; same per-route extension as cors. Rejected.
  - **oauth2 (redirect)** — needs an HTTPS client + auth-state framework. Rejected.
  - **ext_authz / ext_proc** — need a gRPC client (depends on the gRPC family). Rejected.
  - **buffer / bandwidth_limit / compression / response-rate-limit** — need a body-buffering/streaming framework extension larger than the filter. Rejected.
  - **global rate limit** — depends on the gRPC family. Rejected.
  - **adaptive concurrency / admission control** — sophisticated + probabilistic/stats-heavy; non-differential-testable core. Rejected.
  - **lua / wasm** — own multi-phase sub-projects per §9. Rejected.

  Top-3 viable: **fault (abort) > jwt_authn > cors.** **fault (abort) wins** on: zero foundations grant; the lowest-risk vehicle for the 09 M2 H2-decoration close; deterministic abort fully differential-testable; ~1350-1500 LoC under the §6.1 gate. The SPEC §3 D6 records the H2 decoration helper (the centerpiece); SPEC §6.2 makes the empirical verification at state-2 PLAN-write REQUIRED for the fault stats namespace + abort body bytes + H2 abort header set against `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0` (the phase-10-ratified verify-at-PLAN-write process improvement).

- **Scope-cut decision (fractional percentage deferral):** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Phase 11 supports **deterministic 0%/100% percentage only**; the validator rejects fractional (`0 < numerator < denominator`) per SPEC §4 + §5.6. **Rationale:** a fractional per-request abort decision depends on a random draw that cannot be matched across two independent proxies, so it is non-differential-testable per the differential contract §7.2; landing it would be untested (un-differential-verifiable) code per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §6.3. Deferring it (a) keeps phase 11 entirely inside the differential contract, (b) avoids landing a hand-rolled per-request PRNG that no fixture can exercise bilaterally, and (c) keeps the phase under the §6.1 gate. The deterministic 0%/100% path needs no PRNG. Fractional defers to a future fault-enrichment phase that hand-rolls the PRNG per D-3.2 + opts into statistical-distribution unit testing per §7.2 (consistent with the rbac "header-based scope only; IP/CIDR defers" minimum-viable-cut discipline at phase 10).

- **Phase id + slug pick:** **`11`** / **`11-http-filter-fault`**. The id `11` is next sequential after `10` per the project's numbering convention (the next standalone-phase id is the next integer; sub-phases get `.N` suffixes only on split). The slug mirrors the `10-http-filter-rbac` + `09-http-filter-local-rate-limit` hyphenation pattern — readable English (`http-filter-fault`) over the upstream-name verbatim. The filter's typed_config `name:` field uses upstream's exact `envoy.filters.http.fault` (typed_config `@type` `…fault.v3.HTTPFault`); the slug + readable name + upstream config name are deliberately distinct surfaces per the 07.2 / 09 / 10 precedent.

- **depends-on pick:** **`07`** (the parent filter-chain framework). Per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §9 depends-on doctrine (HTTP-filter-family extensions name `07`). **An implicit dependency on phase `05` (the HTTP/2 codec + `Driver::Http2` harness primitive) is load-bearing for the first time in the HTTP-filter family** (phase 11's fixture runs on an H2 listener) — but per ROADMAP schema conventions only direct ROADMAP-row deps are captured in the field; cross-deliverable reuse (05 H2 codec, 04.2 HeaderMatcher, 09 H1 helper template) is implicit. The 17-Docker-gated-fixture regression baseline established at phase-10 close (`0001-tcp-echo` through `0017-http-filter-rbac`) carries forward per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §7.5 (b).

- **Split-gate projection at state-2 (SPEC §6.1):** Single-phase, no split. Per SPEC §3 deliverable enumeration: ~10-12 tasks / ~1350-1500 LoC (production ~620, tests ~700, fixture/harness/doc ~280). At-or-under the §6.1 ~25-task / ~1500-LoC gate; task count comfortably under. **Recommended posture: accept the projection; do NOT nest-split.** Release valve if state-3 drifts over ~1600 LoC: use SPEC §3 D8.1 option (b) (single-probe `Driver::Http2` abort assertion + pass-through covered by the in-process backstop, saving the ~90 LoC `Driver::Http2ProbeList` extension) before any nest-split, per parent-08 SPEC §6.1 alternative (vi).

- **ADR projection at state-1: NONE.** Recommended posture per SPEC §7 — no foundations grants required at any phase-11 lifecycle state (abort reuses existing primitives; `FractionalPercent` is hand-authored envoy-config; the H2 helper is hand-rolled per D-3.2). DECISIONS.md ledger head stays at **ADR-0034** through this state-1 commit; next available **ADR-0035**. Four conditional ADR-0035 slots stay reserved-available for state-2 / state-3 landing (per SPEC §7): option A (PLAN-write §6.2 empirical-verification revision — **the most likely trigger**, given the phase-10 RBAC body was off by 1 byte; the fault abort-body bytes are the likeliest correction); option B (fractional-deferral durability ADR — recommended DEFER; the §4 + §5.6 rationale is self-contained); option C (foundations grant — no grant projected); option D (`Driver::Http2ProbeList` harness-primitive ADR — recommended no-ADR, per the `Driver::Http1ProbeList`/`Driver::AdminScrape` no-ADR harness-extension cadence). At most ONE of A/B/C/D lands at any single commit per D-3.5 sequential numbering.

- **Carryforward closure projection at state-1: 09 REVIEW M2 (implementation arm) closes at phase-11 D6.** Phase 10's D5 closed M2's documentation arm (the ADR-0033 Consequences amendment at `DECISIONS.md:699` naming this phase as the implementation close site). Phase 11's D6 lands the implementation arm: a new `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` helper (~50-70 LoC + 2 tests, the amendment's named size) symmetric to the H1 helper, wired into both H2 HCM synth writer-arm sites (`crates/envoy-http2/src/hcm.rs:373` decode-side `SynthFromDecode` + `:436` encode-side `StopAndSend`), exercised bilaterally by fixture 0018 on an H2 listener. **After D6 lands, the 09 → 10 → 11 M2 chain ENDS.** No new ADR is required to close M2 (the close shape is ordinary implementation deliverable work, not a decision).

- **No other carryforwards engaged in 11 (besides the phase-10 M1 lesson heeded proactively).** Phase-10 M1 (the RBAC backstop omitted the per-probe 5-header presence assertion as an undisclosed deviation) is heeded proactively at phase-11 D8.3 per SPEC §6.4 (the fault backstop SHOULD include the per-probe standard-header presence assertion on the 503 probes, OR disclose its omission). Standing inventory carries forward unchanged: 10 M2 (`hcm.rs:~187` H1 call-site doc-comment — phase 11 widens NO H1 signature, so likely not touched; close opportunistically); 10 M3/M4/D1/D2/T1/T2/T3; 09 REVIEW M1 + T2 (token-bucket CAS race / refill-path coverage — not engaged by fault; no token bucket); 09 REVIEW D1/D2/T1/T3; 08.2 REVIEW M1-M8 + T1-T3; 08.1 REVIEW M3; 07.2 REVIEW M2 + M3; 06.3 REVIEW I2 (upstream-robustness named owner; not engaged); 06.2 REVIEW M1/M2/M4/M5; 06.1 REVIEW M2/M3/M5/M6; 05.3 REVIEW I2; 05.2 REVIEW I1/I2/I3; 04.1 REVIEW M5/M9/M-claim/M1/M2/M4/M7; 02.2 REVIEW M1; Phase-00 I3 SIGKILL→SIGTERM — all carry forward indefinitely per their existing named-owner dispositions.

- **Fixture 0018 projection:** `tests/fixtures/0018-http-filter-fault/` lands at SPEC §3 D8.1 — the **first HTTP-filter-family fixture on an HTTP/2 listener** (the deliberate inverse of the 09 + 10 H1-only fixtures). Bootstrap mirrors fixture 0009's H2 HCM + `direct_response` shape (`codec_type: HTTP2`); fault config `abort: { http_status: 503, percentage: { numerator: 100, denominator: HUNDRED } }` gated by `headers: [- name: x-fault, string_match: { exact: abort }]`. A 4-probe H2 burst alternates the gate header → expected status sequence `[503, 200, 503, 200]`. 503 responses carry the standard H2 headers `{server, date, content-length, content-type}` (NO `connection` — H2-forbidden) via `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` + the abort body byte-exact (exact bytes §6.2-verified at state-2). **Harness:** recommended new `Driver::Http2ProbeList` (mirrors `Driver::Http1ProbeList`; the first multi-probe H2 differential driver) — PLAN-writer confirms the `Driver::Http2` internals + decides between the probe-list driver (recommended; both arms bilateral) or a single-probe abort assertion (the §6.1 trim). Docker-gated wrapper at `tests/differential/tests/http_filter_fault.rs` mirrors the 10 `http_filter_rbac.rs` shape. Fuzz corpus seed `crates/envoy-config/fuzz/corpus/parse_bootstrap/hcm_fault_filter.yaml` extends the corpus 17 → 18 + extends the SUCCESS array per the 09 + 10 Task-6 follow-up precedent. In-process backstop `crates/envoy-bin/tests/http_filter_fault.rs` exercises the H1 abort path (cheap H1-codec coverage to complement the H2 fixture) with the 09 REVIEW M3 `tokio::process::Command + kill_on_drop + Stdio::null` discipline already baked in (M3 closed at phase 10; now the standing pattern).

- **BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md extension projection:** 1 new "Stat-name mapping" row (`http.<hcm_stat_prefix>.fault.aborts_injected` — value-exact; §6.2-verified at state-2) lands at the D7 task commit. No new Header allow-list row needed per SPEC §2.2 (the 04.1-landed `server` + `date` rows cover the cross-proxy implementation-identifying differences; `content-length` + `content-type` are value-exact under the deterministic fixture-0018 burst). Per the 06.x / 07.x / 08.x / 09 / 10 doctrine: contract extensions land at the task where each is first empirically exercised, NOT at PLAN-write time and NOT at state-1 SPEC time.

### Phase-12 state-1 brainstorm

Brainstorm session decisions (THIS commit), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") and `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork" — applies to state-3, not this state-1 controller-run brainstorm). This is the **fourth post-MVP-trunk feature-family brainstorm session** (after phase-09 `3025594` + phase-10 `c73f44f` + phase-11 `1370aaa`; the latter is the freshest shape precedent). The pick was made directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation` — no two-option fork; the alternatives + 5-dimension scoring are documented below.

- **Family pick: Upstream robustness family (FIRST concrete phase; first feature-family phase to LEAVE the HTTP-filter family).** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. **The decisive context shift:** the HTTP-filter family was picked three times running (09/10/11) chiefly to close the 09 REVIEW M2 H2-decoration carryforward — which CLOSED at phase 11 (the H1 + H2 HCM filter-synth writer paths are now at parity). With that driver gone, the pick is free to diversify. Applying the same 5-dimension scoring framework used at phases 09 + 10 + 11:

  - **Upstream robustness family (WINNER) — active HTTP health checking** — (a) carryforward closure **MEDIUM-HIGH** — it is the **named owner of 06.3 REVIEW I2** (the deferred synthetic-backend harness infrastructure; the REVIEW named "the upstream-robustness family" as the close site verbatim). Phase 12 makes the first down-payment (the synthetic health-aware backend); full I2 closure (per-class counter wire coverage + `upstream_cx_total` `value-exact`) remains tied to connection pooling per the REVIEW disposition. (b) foundation pressure **ZERO** — reuses tokio (already permitted) + `envoy-http1::Client` (04.3) for the probe + `envoy-config::parse_duration` + `Int64Range` + `envoy-stats`. (c) arch risk **LOW-MEDIUM** — the integration seam is **PRE-BUILT and annotated for this**: `ClusterHandle::pick_endpoint() -> Option<SocketAddr>` (`cluster.rs:152`, *"preserved for phase-06+ health checking"*) + the existing no-healthy-endpoint → synth-503 path (`router.rs:18-20`, `hcm.rs:580-582`). The genuinely-new surface is the periodic-probe task (the project's first periodic-background primitive) + the `EndpointHealth` state machine + the panic threshold. (d) contract maturity **MEDIUM** — new health-check stats + the no-healthy-upstream body need §6.2 empirical pinning. (e) scope tractability **MEDIUM (upper band; SPLIT projected)** — ~1800-2200 LoC, over the §6.1 gate → likely split into 12.1 + 12.2.
  - **Load balancing family** (`least_request` / `ring_hash` / `random` etc.) — documented "strong second-place" at phases 10 + 11 on the LoC/foundations axes, but **re-scored DOWN this brainstorm on differential-testability**: randomized LBs (`random`, default-P2C `least_request`) produce per-request endpoint picks that CANNOT be matched across two independent proxies (non-differential-testable, same class as fractional fault percentage at phase 11); the deterministic LBs (`ring_hash`, `maglev`) require EXACT hash-algorithm fidelity (xxHash / Maglev permutation) — high arch risk for differential equivalence. Any LB differential test also needs a NEW distinguishable-backend + endpoint-attribution harness primitive. (a) carryforward closure ZERO; (b) zero foundation; (c) LOW-MEDIUM but the differential-observability is fragile; (d) MEDIUM; (e) HIGH LoC tractability. **Rejected** on the differential-testability fragility + zero carryforward closure — a more careful analysis than the prior brainstorms' LoC-focused "second-place" billing.
  - **Continuing HTTP-filter family** (`cors` / `jwt_authn` / `ext_authz` / `csrf` / `compression` / `buffer`) — the low-hanging StopAndSend fruit is EXHAUSTED (header_mutation 07.2, local_ratelimit 09, rbac 10, fault 11 done). The remaining filters each need either a foundations grant (jwt_authn JOSE; oauth2 HTTPS client; compression flate2/brotli), a per-route framework extension (cors/csrf), or the gRPC family (ext_authz/ext_proc/global-rate-limit). So continuing now carries HIGHER foundation pressure / arch risk than before + the decisive 09-M2 driver is gone. **Rejected** — diversifying is the better engineering move.
  - **Network filters family** — HIGH arch risk (new network-layer filter-chain primitive) + LOW contract maturity. Rejected per the 09/10/11 reasoning.
  - **HTTP/3 + QUIC** — VERY HIGH foundation pressure (`quinn`) + VERY HIGH arch risk. Rejected.
  - **gRPC / xDS / Observability / Runtime+hot-restart / WASM / Deprecated** — same dispositions as phases 09/10/11 (unmotivated / highest-risk / multi-phase sub-project / meta-bookkeeping). Rejected.

  Top-3 viable: **Upstream robustness > Load balancing > continuing HTTP filters.** Upstream robustness wins on: closing the named-owner carryforward (06.3 I2); zero foundation pressure; the pre-built integration seam (lowering arch risk); and laying the periodic-task primitive the whole family needs. The SPEC §1 + §3 record the picked family + feature scope.

- **Feature pick: active HTTP health checking (HTTP checker only; minimum-viable).** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Among the family's features (active HC / outlier detection / circuit breakers / retries / connection pooling), active HTTP health checking is the §9-listed-first + the cleanest first phase: response-based differential observable (200-through vs synth-503-no-healthy, deterministic in steady state), reuses the pre-built `pick()->Option` + synth-503 seam, and builds the periodic-probe primitive the family reuses. **Scope cuts (defer per SPEC §4):** TCP/gRPC/custom checkers; multiple health checks per cluster; outlier detection; circuit breakers; retries; connection pooling; `no_traffic_interval`/jitter/`reuse_connection`; event logging; degraded/excluded host states; HC over H2 upstream. HTTP-only + binary Healthy/Unhealthy + single fixed interval + fresh-connection-per-probe is the minimum-viable cut (consistent with the rbac "header-scope only" + fault "deterministic-only" minimum-viable discipline).

- **Phase id + slug pick:** **`12`** / **`12-upstream-active-health-check`**. id `12` is next sequential after `11` (the next standalone-phase id is the next integer; sub-phases get `.N` on split). The slug names the family (`upstream`) + the feature (`active-health-check`) per the readable-English hyphenation pattern (`11-http-filter-fault` / `10-http-filter-rbac` analogue). HTTP-checker scope (TCP/gRPC defer) is conveyed in the title/SPEC, not the slug.

- **depends-on pick:** **`02 04`** — phase `02` (cluster manager + round-robin LB seam being extended) + phase `04` (04.3's `envoy-http1::Client`, reused for the HTTP probe). Phase `06` (`envoy-stats`) is load-bearing for the health-check stats but is implicit (the schema captures the primary direct foundations; cross-deliverable reuse — `envoy-stats`, `parse_duration`, `Int64Range` — is implicit per the phase-11 "implicit 05 dep" precedent).

- **Split-gate projection at state-2 (SPEC §6.1): LIKELY SPLIT into 12.1 + 12.2.** SPEC-time estimate ~1800-2200 LoC (production ~850, tests ~900, fixture/harness/backend ~470) — materially OVER the §6.1 ~1500-LoC gate (task count ~14-16, under ~25). **Recommended seam:** 12.1 = D1 schema + D2 validator + D3 `EndpointHealth` + D5 LB-integration + D6 stats (no new fixture; regression-equivalence via the 18 existing fixtures staying green — the 05.1/07.1 foundation-slice pattern) / 12.2 = D4 active-HC task + the new `envoy-health` crate + D7 fixture 0019 + synthetic backend + settle-driver + backstop + fuzz seed + parent-12 close. The state-2 PLAN-write materializes the LoC estimate and executes the split per §6.2 if confirmed, landing **ADR-0036 (split)**.

- **ADR projection at state-1: NONE.** No ADR lands at this state-1 commit. DECISIONS.md ledger head stays **ADR-0035**; next available **ADR-0036**. Projected state-2 landings: **ADR-0036** (the SPLIT ADR — LIKELY per §6.1); **ADR-0037** (a §6.2 empirical-verification revision — PLAUSIBLE, given the HEAVY 6-item verification surface, esp. the initial-health-state semantics + the no-healthy-upstream body bytes). The `envoy-health` crate creation is recommended as a PLAN lock-in, NOT an ADR (a clean-DAG new crate is ordinary structure, like `envoy-stats`/`envoy-accesslog`/`envoy-filter`). No foundations grant projected.

- **Carryforward engaged: 06.3 REVIEW I2 (down-payment, NOT full closure).** Phase 12's D7.1 lands the first synthetic-backend harness primitive — the infrastructure 06.3 REVIEW I2 named ("whichever phase first surfaces the synthetic backend ... the upstream-robustness family"). **Phase 12 does NOT fully close I2:** the residual (per-class `downstream_rq_3xx/4xx/5xx` + `cluster.<name>.upstream_rq_5xx` wire-level coverage + the `cluster.<name>.upstream_cx_total` `value-exact` tightening) remains tied to connection pooling per the REVIEW §3 disposition. The state-2/state-3 PROGRESS attributes the down-payment honestly (D-3.4). **No other carryforward engaged** — phase 12 touches no HTTP-filter file (the phase-11 M1/M2/M3 doc-drift trio + the rest of the inherited inventory carry forward unchanged).

### Phase-12 state-2 split decision

This subsection records the phase-12 state-2 PLAN-write commit (`phase 12: state-2 split decision + sub-phase SPECs (12.1/12.2) [ADR-0036, ADR-0037]`) — the fifth project SPLIT state-2 (after phases 02/03/07/08), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` (pick the recommendation; no two-option fork) + `feedback_execution_style` (the state-2 PLAN-write is the controller's authoring pass; subagent-driven execution applies at state 3). Docs-only; 5 files; ADR-0036 (split) + ADR-0037 (no-healthy-body reconciliation); no SPEC edit to the parent-12 SPEC; no PLAN/PROGRESS (those land at the 12.1 state-2 PLAN-write next session).

**§6.2 empirical-verification findings (HEAVY; 6 items; against `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0`).** Performed at THIS commit by the controller directly (per the parent SPEC §6.2 + the phase-10/11-ratified verify-at-PLAN-write discipline). Methodology: a Docker bridge network with (a) a synthetic health-aware backend container (returns 200 on `/`, a configurable status on `/healthz`, logs every probe) and (b) an `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0` container running an active-HC cluster pointing at the backend by container name (STRICT_DNS); admin `/stats` scraped + data-plane `GET /` probed across the convergence window. *(Initial host-networking attempts via `host.docker.internal` network-failed because it resolves IPv6-only on this Docker Desktop; resolved by co-locating both containers on a shared bridge network.)*

| # | Item | Finding | Disposition |
|---|------|---------|-------------|
| 1 | **Initial endpoint health state** (the single most load-bearing) | **Unhealthy/pending-until-first-pass.** An unhealthy backend with a 3 s probe delay → data-plane `no healthy upstream` (503) from t=0, *before* the first probe resolves. Hosts with active HC configured begin not-healthy; the first passing check (`healthy_threshold` consecutive successes) marks them healthy. | **MATCHES** the parent SPEC's recommended projection. Drives D3 (`EndpointHealth` initial state = Unhealthy) + the 12.1 inert-and-unexercised-seam note. No ADR. |
| 2 | **No-healthy-upstream synth body** (the byte-precision item) | status **503**; body **`no healthy upstream`** = **19 bytes**, hex `6e 6f 20 68 65 61 6c 74 68 79 20 75 70 73 74 72 65 61 6d`, **no trailing newline**; headers `{ content-length:19, content-type:text/plain, date, server:envoy, connection:close }`; **no wire `x-envoy-*` flag header**. | **DIVERGES from envoy-rust's existing synth-503** (`hcm.rs:918 synth_status` = EMPTY body, content-length:0). → **ADR-0037**: reconcile envoy-rust to emit the 19-byte body on the no-healthy-upstream path (impl in 12.2 D6.2, only the `hcm.rs:582` arm — NOT global `synth_status`). Fixture 0019 asserts byte-exact. |
| 3 | **Panic threshold** | default **50 %**; comparison **strictly-below** (`healthy% < threshold`: `value:0` → `0%<0%`=false → no panic → 503; default 50 % → `0%<50%`=true → panic → 200 routed to all); `Percent` shape **`{ value: <double> }`**; panic = round-robin over ALL; bonus stat `cluster.<n>.lb_healthy_panic` (not wired at phase-12 scope). | **MATCHES** projection. Drives D1 (`CommonLbConfig`/`Percent`) + D5 (panic logic). Fixture uses `{ value: 0 }` (disable panic). No ADR. |
| 4 | **Health-check stat names** | `cluster.<n>.health_check.{attempt,success,failure,passive_failure,network_failure,verify_cluster,healthy,degraded}` + `membership_{healthy,total,degraded,excluded,change}`. `membership_healthy` = count of currently-healthy endpoints (1 healthy / 0 ejected). | **MATCHES** projection. 12.1 wires the minimum-viable subset: `attempt`/`success`/`failure` counters + `membership_healthy` gauge (rest defer per parent §4). No ADR. |
| 5 | **HTTP probe shape** | `GET /healthz`, `:authority`/`Host: <cluster-name>` (default when `http_health_check.host` unset), `user-agent: Envoy/HC`; default `expected_statuses` = **exactly 200** (201 → unhealthy); `Int64Range` **half-open `[start, end)`** (`[200,201)` excludes 201; `[200,202)` includes it — reusable directly). | **MATCHES** projection. Drives D1 (`HttpHealthCheck`) + D4 (probe). No ADR. |
| 6 | **Duration config shape** | Envoy accepts decimal-second protobuf-JSON durations (`1s`/`0.5s`/`0.25s`) but **REJECTS `500ms`**; envoy-rust's `parse_duration` accepts `1s`/`500ms`/`500us` (integer + s/ms/us) but **REJECTS `0.5s`** (parses the numeric part as `u64`). **Shared form = integer seconds only.** | **DIVERGES from the parent SPEC fixture sketch's `interval: 0.5s`** (which envoy-rust would reject). → **PLAN-time correction (no ADR):** the 12.2 fixture uses integer-second durations (`interval: 1s`, `timeout: 1s`) on both sides (identical YAMLs); 12.1 reuses `parse_duration` as-is (no `0.5s` support added — scope creep). |

**Disposition:** items 1/3/4/5 MATCHED → no ADR. Item 2 → **ADR-0037** (the load-bearing wire-level body bytes pinned; the ADR-0034-class durability decision; reconcile-by-implementation per D-3.3). Item 6 → PLAN-time fixture-shape correction (no ADR). DECISIONS.md ledger head **ADR-0035 → ADR-0037** (ADR-0036 split + ADR-0037 reconciliation, consecutive numbers at this commit per the parent SPEC §6.2 "if both fire at the PLAN-write commit").

**Split-gate verdict.** Parent SPEC §6.1 ~1800-2200 LoC, materially over the §6.1 ~1500 gate → **SPLIT into 12.1 + 12.2** (the recommended seam), per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §6.2. Mirrors the parent-07 `6db5a01` + parent-08 `56dee82` 2-way-split shape (the fifth project 2-way split). Parent ROADMAP row 12 → `in-progress` + `sub-phases: 12.1, 12.2`; two sub-phase rows added (`planned`); STATE → `12.1` state 2 (skipping a 12.1 state-1 brainstorm, per the parent-07/08 precedent — the parent SPEC already brainstormed the full surface).

**The split seam:**
- **12.1 (`12.1-endpoint-health-and-lb-integration`)** = D1 schema (`HealthCheck`/`HttpHealthCheck`/`CommonLbConfig`/`Percent`; reuses `parse_duration` + `Int64Range`) + D2 validator (~4-6 `ConfigError` variants) + D3 `EndpointHealth` (envoy-cluster; initial Unhealthy) + D5 LB-integration (`pick()` excludes unhealthy + panic threshold) + D6 stats (`membership_healthy` gauge; the 3 counters land at 12.1 or 12.2 per the PLAN-writer's D6 lock-in) + the `parse_bootstrap` corpus seed (18→19). **No new fixture** — regression-equivalence via the 18 existing fixtures (the 05.1/07.1 foundation-slice pattern; machinery inert when unconfigured). ~900-1000 LoC. **Subtle consequence of the item-1 finding:** in the 12.1-only world a *configured*-HC cluster has no probe task to mark its endpoints healthy, so 12.1 ships NO configured-HC fixture/test (the path stays inert-and-unexercised until 12.2 wires the probe task + fixture together — the 07.1 unused-`StopAndSend` discipline).
- **12.2 (`12.2-active-http-probe-and-fixture`)** = D4 active-HC probe task + the new `envoy-health` crate (clean DAG over envoy-cluster + envoy-http1 + envoy-config + envoy-stats + tokio; no cycle; the project's first periodic-background primitive; reuses `envoy-http1::Client`) + D6.2 the no-healthy-upstream body reconciliation (ADR-0037 impl) + D7.1 fixture 0019 + the synthetic health-aware backend harness primitive (the 06.3 REVIEW I2 down-payment) + the settle-then-probe driver + Docker wrapper + D7.3 in-process backstop (both convergence directions) + parent-12 close. ~1000-1200 LoC.

**The `envoy-health` crate / dependency-cycle decision (parent §5.1):** recommended option A (new `envoy-health` crate, clean DAG, no cycle — a clean-DAG new crate is ordinary structure like `envoy-stats`/`envoy-accesslog`/`envoy-filter`, **no ADR**). Recorded as a 12.2 PLAN lock-in.

**PLAN-time SPEC corrections surfaced at the split (verified against HEAD `edd654c`):** (1) `Cluster` struct at `bootstrap.rs:56` carries `name`/`cluster_type`/`lb_policy`/`load_assignment`/`transport_socket`/`dns_lookup_family`/`typed_extension_protocol_options` (no direct `upstream_protocol` field — it is via `typed_extension_protocol_options`); D1 appends `health_checks` + `common_lb_config`. (2) `parse_duration` (`bootstrap.rs:2289`) is integer-only (rejects `0.5s`) — see item 6. (3) `Int64Range` (`bootstrap.rs:1080`) is half-open `[start, end)` — see item 5. (4) `RouterError::NoHealthyEndpoint` carries `{ cluster: String }` (the SPEC's `router.rs:18-20` is the module-doc anchor; the variant is ~`:26`). (5) `synth_status` (`hcm.rs:918`) currently emits an EMPTY body — the no-healthy-upstream reconciliation (item 2 / ADR-0037) changes only the `hcm.rs:582` arm in 12.2, NOT global `synth_status`. (6) `ConfigError` lives in `crates/envoy-config/src/lib.rs`. (7) `envoy-http1::Client` API = `Client::connect(...)` + `ClientStream::send_request(...)` (`client.rs`). (8) Echo-server helpers at `tests/helpers/{http1,http2,tcp,tls}-echo-server` + `tests/differential/src/backend.rs` (the synthetic-backend helper extends/forks these in 12.2). These corrections carry into the 12.1/12.2 PROGRESS Task 1 preambles.

**Carryforward:** 06.3 REVIEW I2 ENGAGED at 12.2 (synthetic-backend down-payment; NOT full closure — the residual stays with connection pooling). No other carryforward engaged (phase 12 touches no HTTP-filter file). The inherited inventory carries forward unchanged.

**14 PLAN-write SPEC corrections (PLAN §1 — verified against HEAD `67ae869` by direct read of named seams):** (1) runtime `Cluster.endpoint_health` + `Cluster.panic_threshold` are `pub(crate)` — Task 1 lands the `ClusterHandle::health_probe_targets() -> Option<Vec<(SocketAddr, Arc<EndpointHealth>)>>` public accessor (PLAN lock-in #5); (2) runtime `Cluster` does NOT carry the parsed `HealthCheck` config — `envoy-health::Scheduler::spawn(&bootstrap, Arc<ClusterManager>, Arc<StatsRegistry>, CancellationToken)` reads HC config directly from `&bootstrap` + resolved (SocketAddr, EndpointHealth) pairs from `cluster_mgr.get(name).health_probe_targets()`; (3) `envoy_http1::client::Client::connect(addr: SocketAddr, host: &str) -> Result<ClientStream, Http1Error>` at `client.rs:33-47`; `ClientStream::send_request(&mut self, request: Request) -> Result<Response, Http1Error>` at `:73`; (4) both `Client` + `ClientStream` already PUBLIC — no envoy-http1 surface widening needed; (5) `Http1Error::UpstreamConnect { addr, source }` is the connect-failure variant; the probe wraps in `tokio::time::timeout`; (6) `Response.{status: u16, headers: Vec<(String,String)>, body: Bytes}` (`response.rs`); (7) `hcm.rs:582` arm reads `synth_status(503, close)` — the ONLY call-site to change to `synth_no_healthy_upstream(close)`; (8) `synth_status` at `hcm.rs:918` has the 5-standard-header empty-body shape the new helper mirrors modulo body bytes + content-length; (9) `envoy-bin` `from_bootstrap` call-site at `~:124` — Task 1 wires `Scheduler::spawn(&bootstrap, cluster_mgr, registry, token.clone())` after this site; `scheduler.shutdown().await` at the runtime shutdown tail; (10) `StatsRegistry::register_counter(&str) -> Result<Arc<Counter>, StatsError>` (`registry.rs:45`); `Counter::inc()`; idempotent re-register; (11) `Int64Range { start: i64, end: i64 }` half-open `[start, end)` (`bootstrap.rs:1080`); the probe's success check uses `(r.start..r.end).contains(&(status as i64))`; (12) `parse_duration` is `pub fn parse_duration(s: &str) -> Result<Duration, String>` (`bootstrap.rs:2289`; integer-only; rejects `0.5s`); the 12.1 D2 validator already rejects sub-second decimals at parse — the `Scheduler::spawn` re-parse is defense-in-depth; (13) the existing `Http1EchoBackend` at `backend.rs:179` uses a `cargo run`-equivalent subprocess pattern — `HealthAwareHttp1Backend` (Task 4) mirrors this shape with a new helper binary at `tests/helpers/health-aware-http1-backend/`; (14) `Driver` enum at `tests/differential/src/lib.rs:~140` for the `Http1AfterSettle` variant + the port-template `"PORT"` group at `~:1655-1667`.

### Phase-13 state-1 brainstorm

**Family pick.** `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §9 enumerates the eight feature families. At the post-12.2 close-out boundary the slate is fully open — all MVP-trunk phases 00-08 + the three HTTP-filter-family phases (09 `local_ratelimit`, 10 `rbac`, 11 `fault`) + the first Upstream-robustness-family phase (parent-12; sub-phases 12.1 + 12.2) all stand `done`. The candidates for the next phase were: (i) continue the Upstream-robustness family with `13-outlier-detection` (per-endpoint ejection on consecutive 5xx / per-host success-rate thresholds — natural family-list order after active HC) OR `13-connection-pooling` (per-protocol upstream connection pools — the named-owner phase for 06.3 REVIEW I2's residual); (ii) enter a new family (HTTP/3+QUIC, gRPC, xDS, Observability, Runtime+hot-restart, WASM-host).

**Five-dimension scoring:**

| Dimension | `13-outlier-detection` | `13-connection-pooling` | new family (HTTP/3+QUIC, gRPC, xDS, Observability, Runtime, WASM) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Carryforward closure value** | Zero (no named carryforward closes). Outlier detection is a fresh subsystem; would reuse the 12.1 `EndpointHealth` ejection seam but doesn't close anything from the standing inventory. | **HIGH.** Connection pooling is the **named-owner phase for 06.3 REVIEW I2 residual** per the 06.3 REVIEW.md §3 disposition + the 12.2 down-payment scoping. Closes BOTH (a) per-class `downstream_rq_3xx/4xx/5xx` + `cluster.<name>.upstream_rq_5xx` wire bilateral coverage AND (b) `cluster.<name>.upstream_cx_total` `value-exact` tightening. This is the single highest-value closure currently available in the standing inventory. | Zero direct closures. Some families surface their own internal carryforwards (e.g. Observability would tighten the StatsTagExtractor allow-list residual) but none close the 06.3 I2 residual. |
| **Foundation pressure (new top-level Cargo deps)** | LOW. Reuses the 12.1 `EndpointHealth` ejection seam + the 12.2 periodic-task primitive. No new crate. No ADR. | **LOW.** Reuses the 12.2 `HealthAwareHttp1Backend` harness primitive (extend in place) + the existing `ConnGaugeGuard` RAII pattern + `envoy-http1::Client` + `envoy-http2::Client`. Pool primitives live inside `envoy-http1`/`envoy-http2` (`pool.rs` modules). No new top-level Cargo dep. No new ADR projected at SPEC time (conditional ADR-0038 split LIKELY at PLAN-write; conditional ADR-0039 §6.2 revision PLAUSIBLE). | HIGH (variable). HTTP/3+QUIC needs `quinn` foundations grant; gRPC needs additional `tonic` features; xDS needs from-scratch state-machine atop `tonic` transport; Observability needs OTLP/Datadog/XRay; Runtime+hot-restart is significant new infrastructure; WASM-host is its own multi-phase sub-project. Each new family carries a foundations-grant ADR. |
| **Architectural risk** | MEDIUM. The passive-vs-active distinction is subtle (outlier detection observes data-plane responses; doesn't probe); needs careful interaction with the 12-active path. Cross-proxy timing-dependent ejection windows are hard to make deterministic for the differential harness (would need §Timing tolerances opt-in). | **MEDIUM-HIGH.** Pool primitives are the most-complex piece — H2 multiplexed-streams pool with peer-SETTINGS coordination + GOAWAY handling. But the surface is well-understood (Envoy's pool model is documented) + the per-protocol crate placement avoids cycles cleanly. The `upstream_cx_total` increment-site migration (from per-call to per-connection at pool create) is mechanical but must be atomic to avoid double-counting. The cycle-resolution decision (§5.1) is well-anticipated. | HIGH (variable). HTTP/3 introduces a new downstream listener type + a new conformance suite. gRPC complicates the H2 path. xDS introduces dynamic-config state. WASM is a full ABI + engine binding effort. |
| **Contract-surface maturity** | The `outlier_detection.*` Envoy stat tree is documented but timing-dependent. Differential observability is largely stats-based (which counter increments on which trigger) + ejection-window-bounded — harder than connection-pooling's deterministic-pool-state observable. | **HIGH.** The `cluster.<name>.upstream_cx_*` stat tree is fully documented + matures cleanly under pooling. Per-class HCM counters are already 06.3-defined and BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT-locked; phase 13 just realizes them at fixture level. The synthetic-backend pattern is 12.2-precedented. | Variable. xDS has the most mature contract (ADS state machine + effective-config diff). Observability and Runtime have moderate maturity. WASM is least-mature (ABI conformance suite still evolving upstream). |
| **First-phase scope tractability** | Tractable as a single phase if scoped to consecutive-5xx ejection only (split-gate plausible at PLAN-write but not certain). Reuses 12.x infrastructure heavily. | **MEDIUM.** ~1800-2400 LoC projected — over the §6.1 ~1500 gate. SPLIT into 13.1 + 13.2 LIKELY at PLAN-write (recommended seam: H1-only / H2-only, alternative seam: foundation-slice). Each sub-phase comes in at ~1000-1200 LoC each — manageable. | Variable. xDS family is its own multi-phase sub-project at minimum. Observability could fit as ~3 sub-phases. Runtime+hot-restart is moderate. WASM is multi-phase by design. HTTP/3+QUIC is moderate (probably 2 sub-phases — downstream H3 + upstream H3). |

**Decision: `13-connection-pooling`.** Per `feedback_pick_recommendation` (always pick the recommendation; no two-option fork). The recommendation is unambiguously connection pooling because: (1) it's the **named-owner phase for the highest-value standing carryforward** (06.3 REVIEW I2 residual, deferred from 06.3 and named again at 12.2); (2) it **reuses the freshest landed harness primitive** (12.2's `HealthAwareHttp1Backend`); (3) its scope is **tractable with a known SPLIT seam** (H1-only / H2-only at sub-phase 13.1/13.2); (4) the **foundation pressure is LOW** (no new top-level Cargo dep; pool primitives are protocol-internal); (5) it **continues the Upstream-robustness family** in a natural way (active HC at 12 → pooling at 13; outlier detection + circuit breakers + retries defer to follow-up phases — all reuse the 12.x + 13.x infrastructure when they land). The decision is **NOT** "outlier detection" — outlier detection has zero carryforward closure value and timing-dependent observability that the differential harness handles less cleanly; it defers to a follow-up Upstream-robustness phase. The decision is **NOT** "enter a new family" — the project's standing posture (per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §9 family ordering + the established Carryforward-closure-value-first heuristic from the 11/12 brainstorms) favors closing named residuals before opening new families when both are tractable.

**SPLIT projection at state-2.** The SPEC §6.1 LoC estimate (~1800-2400) is materially over the §6.1 ~1500-LoC gate. **The state-2 PLAN-writer is expected to SPLIT phase 13 into `13.1` + `13.2`** at the standalone-PLAN-write commit, mirroring the parent-12 split `4f9ba04` (ADR-0036) + the parent-08 / parent-07 / parent-03 / parent-02 split precedents. **Recommended seam:** `13.1` = H1 pool primitive + envoy-config `circuit_breakers.thresholds` schema + H1 router-arm pool integration + the configurable-status synthetic-backend harness extension + fixture 0020 (H1-only; per-class counter bilateral coverage); `13.2` = H2 pool primitive + H2 router-arm pool integration + the `cluster.<name>.upstream_cx_total` BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT row tightening to `value-exact` (the 06.3 REVIEW I2 (b) full-closure site — lands when both protocols pool) + parent-13 close. ### Phase-13 state-2 split decision

State-2 split commit (THIS commit) — `phase 13: state-2 split decision + sub-phase SPECs (13.1/13.2) [ADR-0038]` — the SIXTH project SPLIT state-2 (after phases 02/03/07/08/12), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") + `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork"; N/A at state-2 PLAN-write — the PLAN-write is the controller's authoring pass). Docs-only; 6 files; ADR-0038 (split decision) only. No SPEC edit to the parent-13 SPEC (preserved verbatim; the sub-phase SPECs carve from it). No PLAN/PROGRESS/REVIEW at THIS commit (those land at the 13.1 state-2 PLAN-write next session and onward). No BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md change (contract extensions land at the 13.1/13.2 state-3 tasks per the 06.x → 12.x cadence).

**§6.2 empirical-verification findings (HEAVY; 9 items; against `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0`).** Performed at THIS commit by the controller directly (per the parent SPEC §6.2 + the phase-10/11/12-ratified verify-at-PLAN-write discipline). Methodology: a Docker bridge network (`p13net`) with (a) a Python-based configurable HTTP/1.1 backend container (Python 3.12-alpine; `BaseHTTPRequestHandler` with `protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"` for keep-alive support; serves 200 on `/`, 301/404/500/503 on `/301`/`/404`/`/500`/`/503`, configurable status on `/healthz` via `HEALTHZ_STATUS` env) and (b) an `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0` container running an HTTP/1.1 HCM listener routing through a STRICT_DNS cluster with `circuit_breakers: { thresholds: [{ priority: DEFAULT, max_connections: 2 }] }` pointing at the backend container by container name; admin `/stats` + `/config_dump` scraped + data-plane requests driven from the host via curl + Python `http.client.HTTPConnection`. *(Initial container-name resolution attempts via `backend` failed because Docker's DNS resolves the container's full name `p13-backend`; resolved by updating the YAML.)* For item (vi) — H2 cluster stats namespace — booted a second Envoy with `typed_extension_protocol_options.envoy.extensions.upstreams.http.v3.HttpProtocolOptions.explicit_http_config.http2_protocol_options: {}` and scraped the cluster stat namespace (no H2C backend reach needed; the stat names are registered at cluster-construct time).

| # | Item | Finding | Disposition |
|---|------|---------|-------------|
| i | **`circuit_breakers.thresholds` shape + max_connections default** | `circuit_breakers: { thresholds: [{ priority?: <RoutingPriority>, max_connections?: u32, max_pending_requests?: u32, max_requests?: u32, max_retries?: u32, ... }] }`. The `priority` field defaults to `DEFAULT` (and is omitted from `/config_dump` when DEFAULT). `max_connections` default 1024 (per Envoy docs; not observed empirically because the harness load is below the cap). | **MATCHES** SPEC projection. Schema D1 lands as projected (`Cluster.circuit_breakers: Option<CircuitBreakers>` with `Thresholds` carrying `priority?: Option<RoutingPriority>` + `max_connections?: Option<u32>`; phase-13 supports DEFAULT + max_connections only; other threshold fields rejected by `deny_unknown_fields`). No ADR. |
| ii | **H1 default pool behavior under NO `circuit_breakers`** | Envoy ALWAYS pools regardless of `circuit_breakers` config (a no-`circuit_breakers` cluster + 5 requests over a single downstream keep-alive conn → `upstream_cx_total: 1`; full pool reuse). `circuit_breakers` is OPTIONAL at config-time; absence means defaults. `/config_dump` shows `circuit_breakers: "<absent>"` when not configured (Envoy doesn't echo defaults). | **MATCHES** SPEC projection. § 5.4 default-enabled-pool decision: phase-13's H1 pool runs **with hardcoded defaults when `circuit_breakers` is absent** (NOT pool-gated by the field). The 19 existing fixtures (none configures `circuit_breakers`) pool transparently. Regression-equivalence guaranteed by the existing presence-only `upstream_cx_total` assertions (no value-exact assertion to regress). No ADR. |
| iii | **H1 idle_timeout knob + default** | Knob lives at `typed_extension_protocol_options.envoy.extensions.upstreams.http.v3.HttpProtocolOptions.common_http_protocol_options.idle_timeout` (a Duration; nested deep). NOT at the cluster top-level. Default: 1 hour (3600s) per upstream Envoy docs. /config_dump shows `<absent>` for the nested field when not explicitly set. | **MATCHES** SPEC projection. §4 deferral: phase-13 (13.1 + 13.2) does NOT add the config-side `idle_timeout` knob; the pool uses a hardcoded sensible default (60s — short enough to test idle eviction in a deterministic test, long enough to not fire under fixture settle windows). The config-side knob defers to a follow-up phase. No ADR. |
| iv | **`upstream_cx_total` increment semantics + discriminating differential observable** | One increment per established upstream TCP connection. Discriminating observable shape: with a **SINGLE downstream keep-alive conn** issuing 5 sequential requests → `upstream_cx_total: 1` (full pool reuse via 1 upstream connection). With **separate downstream conns** (curl invocations) → `upstream_cx_total: N` (one upstream connect per downstream-side conn — Envoy's H1 pool returns the connection to idle AFTER the downstream conn closes, by which point the next downstream conn has already triggered a fresh upstream connect). Concurrent 4 curl jobs with `max_connections: 2` → `upstream_cx_total: 4` + `upstream_cx_overflow: 2` (max_connections is ADVISORY: Envoy increments the overflow counter but still completes the request). | **MATCHES** SPEC projection but with **CRITICAL NUANCE**. **PLAN-time fixture-shape lock-in:** fixture 0020 driver MUST issue multiple requests over a SINGLE downstream keep-alive conn to make `upstream_cx_total` the discriminating differential observable (1 vs. N). The differential harness's existing `Driver::Http1` opens one conn per request — **13.1 D10 extends the driver with `Driver::Http1KeepAlive`** (~30 LoC; mirrors 12.2's `Driver::Http1AfterSettle` precedent). NOT a wire-contract change → NO ADR fires. |
| v | **`upstream_cx_destroy` + sibling counter names + fire-sites** | Full sibling set per-cluster: `upstream_cx_destroy`, `_destroy_local`, `_destroy_remote`, `_destroy_with_active_rq`, `_destroy_local_with_active_rq`, `_destroy_remote_with_active_rq`, `upstream_cx_close_notify` (counts peer-initiated Connection: close). All fire when a pool connection is destroyed (different sub-counters for local-initiated vs remote-initiated destruction; the per-`_with_active_rq` variants fire only when destruction happens while a request was outstanding). All per-cluster (NOT per-endpoint). | **MATCHES** SPEC projection. D7 (stats): phase-13 wires the parent `upstream_cx_destroy` (always-fire sibling) + `upstream_cx_http1_total` (H1 pool connect counter) + the 13.2-landed `upstream_cx_http2_total`. The 5 sub-siblings defer to follow-up phases (no fixture deterministically forces local-vs-remote-destroy at phase-13 scope). No ADR. |
| vi | **H2 pool default behavior + max_concurrent_streams + multi-conn per endpoint** | H2 cluster stat namespace (no H2 traffic needed; Envoy registers at cluster-construct time): `cluster.<n>.upstream_cx_http1_total` (always registered; even on H2 cluster — for cross-protocol fallback), `upstream_cx_http2_total` (H2 partition), `upstream_cx_http3_total` (H3 — registered for future use). Default `max_concurrent_streams`: Envoy-as-client honors peer's `SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS` (default 100 per RFC 7540 §6.5.2 if peer doesn't send SETTINGS). Per-endpoint multi-conn threshold: Envoy spawns 2nd upstream H2 conn only when 1st is at peer's cap. Legacy cluster-field `http2_protocol_options` stays absent in `/config_dump` when `typed_extension_protocol_options` is used (Envoy's modern path). | **MATCHES** SPEC projection. 13.2 D5 H2 pool primitive design: per-endpoint connection list with `active_streams: AtomicU32` tracking + spawn 2nd conn only when 1st at peer's `SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS` cap (or cluster's `max_connections` cap, whichever is lower). 13.2 D7.2 lands the `cluster.<n>.upstream_cx_http2_total` BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT row. No ADR. |
| vii | **Per-class HCM counters under synthetic 5xx backend fixture** | Drove 6 requests through a single downstream keep-alive conn: 2× GET /(200), 1× GET /301(301), 1× GET /404(404), 1× GET /500(500), 1× GET /503(503). HCM scrape: `http.ingress_http.downstream_rq_2xx: 2`, `_3xx: 1`, `_4xx: 1`, `_5xx: 2`, `_total: 6`. Listener-namespace mirror: `listener.0.0.0.0_10000.http.ingress_http.downstream_rq_{2,3,4,5}xx` — same values. Bilateral byte-exact achievable under deterministic load. | **MATCHES** SPEC projection. 13.1 D9.1 fixture 0020 lands the per-class workload; the existing BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT rows at `:96-100` (06.3-landed `value-exact`) are EXERCISED bilaterally at the wire level for the FIRST time at this fixture — the **06.3 REVIEW I2 (a) full-closure site**. No ADR. |
| viii | **`cluster.<name>.upstream_rq_5xx` + siblings** | Same 6-request workload: cluster scrape `cluster.backend_cluster.upstream_rq_2xx: 2`, `_3xx: 1`, `_4xx: 1`, `_5xx: 2`, `_total: 6`. Cluster-namespace per-class counters match HCM-namespace per-class counters byte-exact under deterministic load (because router-proxy passes through every upstream status). | **MATCHES** SPEC projection. Fixture 0020 also asserts the cluster-namespace per-class values (a second I2 (a) closure surface). The existing BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT row at `:106` (06.3-landed `upstream_rq_5xx: value-exact`) is EXERCISED bilaterally at the wire level here for the first time. No ADR. |
| ix | **HCM filter-synth bypass on per-class counters** | **CONFIRMED.** Shut down backend → GET / → synth-503 `no healthy upstream` (body verified byte-exact = ADR-0037's 19 bytes; bonus phase-12 ADR-0037 bilateral re-confirmation). HCM `downstream_rq_5xx`: 2 → 3 (synth INCLUDED at HCM); HCM `downstream_rq_total`: 6 → 7. Cluster `upstream_rq_5xx`: 2 → 2 (synth NOT INCLUDED at cluster); Cluster `upstream_rq_total`: 6 → 6 (synth bypasses upstream → no upstream counter increment). | **MATCHES** the existing 06.3 BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT note at `:96-106` exactly ("the per-class counter is symmetric on `response_status_for_log`, agnostic to synth-vs-proxy origin" applies at HCM; cluster-level upstream_rq_* is upstream-only). Phase-13 preserves the bypass unchanged. **Bonus: ADR-0037's 19-byte `no healthy upstream` body bilaterally re-confirmed in passing** (a useful re-validation of the 12.2 implementation). No ADR. |

**§6.2 synthesis: ALL 9 items MATCHED the parent-13 SPEC's projections.** No empirical-revision ADR fires at the state-2 split commit. The §6.2 conditional ADR slot (parent-13 SPEC §7 option B; reserved as `ADR-0039`) does NOT consume `ADR-0039` — the slot stays reserved-available for future phase use. The item-(iv) discriminating-observable nuance is a PLAN-time fixture-shape lock-in (use `Driver::Http1KeepAlive`), NOT a wire-contract change. The item-(iii) finding is a §4 deferral (idle_timeout knob defers; pool uses hardcoded 60s default).

**Split decision lock-in (per `feedback_pick_recommendation`):**
- **Seam choice:** H1-only / H2-only protocol-cohesion seam (the SPEC §6.1 recommended posture). Alternative foundation-slice seam (H1+H2 pool primitives in 13.1, router-arm wiring in 13.2) was rejected because the §6.2 item-(ii) default-enabled-pool finding breaks the inert-when-unconfigured property the 05.1/07.1/12.1 foundation-slice pattern relies on (a pool primitive landed without router-arm integration creates a half-wired seam that runs on every existing fixture with hardcoded defaults but doesn't change any observable behavior because the router-arm still uses per-call connect — unnecessary complexity vs. the H1-only/H2-only split where each sub-phase is atomically wired per protocol).
- **13.1 scope:** D1 envoy-config `circuit_breakers` schema + D2 validator + D3 H1Pool primitive (`crates/envoy-http1/src/pool.rs`) + D4 H1 router-arm pool integration (modify `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs:514` — the verified increment site, NOT `router.rs:85-90` as the parent SPEC §8 incorrectly stated) + D7-H1 pool stats wiring (cluster.<n>.upstream_cx_destroy + upstream_cx_http1_total; NO upstream_cx_total tightening yet) + D8 configurable-backend extension (additive --per-path on 12.2 helper) + D9.1 fixture 0020 (per-class counter bilateral coverage = 06.3 REVIEW I2 (a) full-closure) + D9.3 in-process H1 backstop + D10 Driver::Http1KeepAlive harness extension + D11 fuzz seed `cluster_circuit_breakers.yaml` (corpus 20→21). ~1000-1200 LoC. The named full-closure site for **06.3 REVIEW I2 (a)**.
- **13.2 scope:** D5 H2Pool primitive (`crates/envoy-http2/src/pool.rs`; per-connection active_streams tracking + multi-conn-per-endpoint at peer's SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS cap) + D6 H2 router-arm pool integration (modify `crates/envoy-http2/src/hcm.rs:280`+`:291` — the verified increment sites) + D7.1 BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT `cluster.<name>.upstream_cx_total` row tightening to `value-exact` for H1+H2 (TCP-proxy carved out — TCP pool defers; the named full-closure site for **06.3 REVIEW I2 (b)**) + D7.2 `cluster.<name>.upstream_cx_http2_total` BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT row + D9.1-H2 fixture 0021-upstream-h2-connection-pooling (sibling — reuses existing `http2-echo-server` helper) + D9.3-H2 in-process H2 backstop + parent-13 close (flips ROADMAP rows `13.2` AND parent-13 `13` `in-progress → done` SIMULTANEOUSLY per the closing-sub-phase invariant; mirrors the 02.2/03.2/07.2/08.2/12.2 precedent). ~900-1100 LoC. The named full-closure site for **06.3 REVIEW I2 (b)** + parent-13 close + the full 06.3 REVIEW I2 closure at parent-13 close.
- **Cycle-resolution decision (carried to both sub-phases):** the H1 + H2 pools live in `envoy-http1` + `envoy-http2` respectively (no cross-crate dep change). Each `Cluster` (in `envoy-cluster`) gets `Option<Arc<H1Pool>>` + `Option<Arc<H2Pool>>` fields injected at `envoy-bin` startup (the bin-wired injection pattern; mirrors 12.2's `envoy-health::Scheduler` wiring). No new trait in `envoy-cluster`; no new top-level Cargo dep; no cycle. Recommended posture: NO ADR (the injection is ordinary structure). The 13.1/13.2 PLAN-writers verify the seam shape at PLAN-time.
- **TCP-proxy carve-out (13.2 D7.1 narrative):** the existing TCP-proxy increment site at `crates/envoy-tcp/src/lib.rs:108` is UNTOUCHED at phase 13 (TCP pool defers to a follow-up phase). The 13.2 D7.1 BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT row tightening explicitly carves out TCP clusters: "`value-exact` for H1+H2 clusters under the harness's single-downstream-keep-alive-conn driver; `name-required, value-may-differ` for TCP-proxy clusters until TCP pooling lands". Existing TCP fixtures (`0001/0003/0004/0005/0006`) carry the pre-13.2 disposition; their presence-only `upstream_cx_total` assertions are trivially satisfied under the tightened H1+H2 row.

### Phase-13.1 state-2 PLAN-write

State-2 standalone PLAN-write session (THIS commit), per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §5 state 2 + `SKILL_ROUTING.md` + `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("pick the recommendation; do not ask"). Mirrors the 12.1 state-2 PLAN-write `2ac2356` + 12.2 state-2 PLAN-write `6a3b332` verbatim for the file-set shape: CREATE PLAN.md + CREATE PROGRESS.md skeleton + Task 1 preamble + MODIFY ROADMAP row `13.1` `planned → in-progress` + MODIFY STATE.md 4-top-pointer rewrites + append THIS subsection.

**PLAN shape:** 10 TDD tasks; 18 architecture lock-ins; full `- [ ]` checkbox steps with code per task. ~1000-1200 LoC projected for state-3 production code (comfortably under the `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §6.1 ~1500-LoC gate — the parent-13 split (ADR-0038) already absorbed the over-gate scope into 13.1 + 13.2). NO-nest-SPLIT standalone PLAN. Task ordering: Tasks 1-2 envoy-config schema + validator; Task 3 H1Pool primitive (`crates/envoy-http1/src/pool.rs`) unit-tested in isolation; Task 4 H1 HCM proxy-arm migration + tier-1 micro-cache removal + `cx_total` increment-site migration (the load-bearing integration task); Task 5 stats wiring + BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT rows (2 new — `upstream_cx_destroy` + `upstream_cx_http1_total`; NO `upstream_cx_total` row tightening at 13.1 — defers to 13.2 D7.1 per lock-in #3); Task 6 backend extension (`--per-path` flag); Task 7 fixture 0020 + `Driver::Http1KeepAlive` atomic per lock-in #4 (the 06.3 REVIEW I2 (a) full-closure site); Task 8 in-process H1 backstop; Task 9 fuzz seed (corpus 20→21 SUCCESS-array entries); Task 10 state-4 verification + STATE advance.

**Cycle-resolution decision (PLAN lock-in #1) — picked per `feedback_pick_recommendation`:** the 13.1 SPEC §5.1 left the seam open between (a) a new trait declared in `envoy-cluster` (`H1ClientPool` trait implemented by `envoy-http1::H1Pool`; held as `Cluster.h1_pool: Option<Arc<dyn H1ClientPool>>`) vs (b) bin-wired injection via an external `H1PoolManager` registry sibling-to-`ClusterManager`. PLAN-time PICK: option (b). Rationale: (1) option (a) requires interior mutability on `Cluster` (the `Arc<Cluster>` from `from_bootstrap` has no setter), OR widening `from_bootstrap`'s signature to take a `pool_builder` callback, OR chicken-and-egg pre-building with `Cluster.cx_total` registration — all intrusive. (2) option (b) parallels the 12.2 `envoy-health::Scheduler` precedent verbatim — `envoy-bin` builds `cluster_mgr` first via `from_bootstrap`, then `H1PoolManager::for_bootstrap` second (walks bootstrap clusters; looks up each in `cluster_mgr` to grab shared `Arc<Counter>`/`Arc<Gauge>` stat handles; builds one `H1Pool` per H1 cluster); the HCM proxy arm gets the pool manager plumbed alongside `cluster_mgr` at HCM-config construction. (3) NO new trait in `envoy-cluster`; only `ConnGaugeGuard` gains a public `from_gauge` constructor (4-line addition) so pools can construct guards against the shared cluster gauge handle. (4) NO new top-level Cargo dep (per lock-in #9). (5) NO ADR fires (per lock-in #16). If state-3 execution surfaces a non-obvious lifecycle complication (e.g. the HCM-config-construction site has poor ergonomic reach to `Arc<H1PoolManager>`), the controller documents the surfacing finding in PROGRESS + lands an inline ADR resolving it (NOT projected).

**Headline PLAN-time SPEC corrections (11-item list; all verified at state-2 against HEAD `a88fe26` by direct read of each named seam):**

1. **`Cluster` struct at `crates/envoy-cluster/src/cluster.rs:32-76`** has no `h1_pool` field — and 13.1 deliberately does NOT add one (lock-in #1 picks external `H1PoolManager` instead).
2. **`ConnGaugeGuard` at `cluster.rs:18-26`** confirmed; gains a public `from_gauge(Arc<envoy_stats::Gauge>) -> Self` constructor at Task 3 Step 2 (the only envoy-cluster touch at 13.1).
3. **`envoy-http1::Client::connect` at `client.rs:33`** confirmed `pub async fn connect(addr: SocketAddr, host: &str) -> Result<ClientStream, Http1Error>`.
4. **`ClientStream` at `client.rs:56`** has `pub(crate)` fields (`stream`, `host`, `buf`) — sibling `pool.rs` accesses directly; NO visibility widening needed (lock-in #10).

**Task ordering (8 tasks):**

NO `unsafe` introduced. NO ADR landed (the carryforward closures + the joint H1+H2 architecture were SPEC §7 + PLAN lock-in #17 projected — no foundations grant; no wire-contract revision).

- `ae8d7cf` — **Task 1 fold-in** (state-5-style code-quality CRITICAL closure landed inline at state-3): closed the H2 invalidate-Drop TOCTOU race surfaced by the code-quality reviewer. Pre-fix, `H2PoolGuard::Drop` ran `active_streams.fetch_sub(1)` OUTSIDE the `connections.lock()`, opening a window where a concurrent Phase-1 walker in `acquire()` could CAS-claim the stream slot on the to-be-evicted entry, leading to orphaned PoolGuards with broken `established` accounting and falsely-fired `cx_destroy`. Post-fix the invalidate branch takes `connections.lock()` BEFORE the fetch_sub and holds it across both the decrement and the `retain()`. The non-invalidate branch keeps the original lock-free fetch_sub (residual race benign by the sweeper's `active_streams != 0` early-return invariant; documented in a correctness-argument comment block). Also added an H1 race-regression stress test `pool_acquire_after_concurrent_release_1000_iterations_zero_spurious_overflows` (probabilistic 1000-iter exercise of the pre-fix race window; post-fix produces 0 spurious overflows; runs in ~90ms). 3 files touched (+144 / -16 LoC net).

- `07006b4` — **Task 2**: H2 router-arm pool integration. Replaced `pub type HCMConfig = Http1HCMConfig;` at `crates/envoy-http2/src/hcm.rs:27` with proper `pub struct HCMConfig { pub inner: Arc<Http1HCMConfig>, pub h2_pool_mgr: Option<Arc<crate::pool::H2PoolManager>> }` + `wrap` constructor (lock-in #2). Migrated H2 dispatch arm at `hcm.rs:286-296` from per-call `crate::Client::connect` to `config.h2_pool_mgr.get(&cluster_name).acquire(endpoint, &host_header).await` (the H1-cluster-in-H2-HCM arm at `:273-284` STAYS UNTOUCHED per lock-in #7 — unusual cross-protocol config). Outer `_cx_guard` at `hcm.rs:268` relocated from unconditional to `Option<ConnGaugeGuard>` Some-only on the H1 arm (lock-in #8 — mirrors 13.1 Task 4 code-quality fold-in pattern). envoy-bin wired `H2PoolManager::for_bootstrap` between `H1PoolManager::for_bootstrap` and `envoy-health::Scheduler::spawn`; HTTP2 codec-dispatch site wraps via `HCMConfig::wrap`. New integration TDD test `h2_hcm_pool_reuses_upstream_conn_across_sequential_requests` (3 sequential requests → cx_total = 1). 3 files touched (+378 / -34 LoC net).

- `ef6deda` — **Task 2 fold-in** (state-5-style code-quality IMPORTANT closure landed inline at state-3): corrected a misleading comment block at the `_cx_guard` relocation site that claimed the H2-arm pool-None fallthrough "matches the 13.1 H1 HCM's `OneShot`-arm semantic (cx_active does not fire)". Both halves of that claim were factually wrong: (i) the H1 OneShot arm at `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs:622-624` DOES fire `cx_active_guard()`; (ii) the pre-13.2 H2 arm DID hold an unconditional outer guard (removed by Task 2's diff). The correct framing: H2-arm pool-wired (production) preserves cx_active via the inner PoolGuard's ConnGaugeGuard; H2-arm pool-None (test paths only) is a behavior change — cx_active does NOT fire — but no existing test asserts cx_active during in-flight H2 dispatch on the test path, so no test breakage. Comment + PROGRESS narrative rewritten honestly. 2 files touched (+~30 LoC net).

- **Multi-guard double-invalidate accounting MINOR** (surfaced by Task 1 code-quality reviewer; pre-existing in the H2 pool): if two H2 PoolGuards are concurrently held against the same multiplexed entry and BOTH are subsequently invalidated, the second invalidate-Drop hits a `retain` no-op but still decrements `established` AGAIN + fires `cx_destroy` AGAIN — a double-count. Tracked as a Minor for a future phase; not in scope for state-5 review of 13.2.
- **Discriminating-power gap at H2 fixture + backstop:** `upstream_cx_total: 1` + `upstream_cx_http2_total: 1` prove H2 pool conn-reuse but do NOT independently discriminate stream-multiplex from a serial acquire-release cycle on one pooled conn. Named at the in-code surface of `crates/envoy-bin/tests/upstream_h2_connection_pooling.rs` (Task 6 fold-in) + in PROGRESS. The state-5 reviewer may surface a stronger in-process observable if one becomes available; otherwise the gap accepts as a known follow-up.
- **Pre-existing differential-suite local-flake patterns:** `access_log_file_sink` (Envoy v1.33 file-access-log writer wall-time race; the 13.1 state-5 fold-in's structural mitigation reduces but doesn't eliminate variance under loaded Docker) + 2 differential lib tests (kernel-port-contention; pass in isolation). ### Phase-14 state-1 brainstorm

**Family pick.** At the post-parent-13-close boundary (`96630f9`) the slate was fully open — all MVP-trunk phases 00-08 + the three HTTP-filter-family phases (09 `local_ratelimit`, 10 `rbac`, 11 `fault`) + the first two Upstream-robustness-family phases (parent-12; sub-phases 12.1 + 12.2 + parent-13; sub-phases 13.1 + 13.2) all stand `done`. The standing posture options enumerated in the previous-session "Next expected skill" block (STATE.md before THIS commit) were: **(A)** continued Upstream-robustness — outlier detection / circuit breakers expansion / retries+hedging / TCP-proxy connection pooling; **(B)** HTTP/3 + QUIC new family; **(C)** gRPC new family; **(D)** xDS / dynamic config new family; **(E)** Observability new family; **(F)** Runtime + hot restart new family; **(G)** WASM host new family; **(H)** ADR-0028 closure as a foundations-pivot phase. Per `feedback_pick_recommendation` (always pick the recommendation; no two-option fork), the controller picked **(A) continued Upstream-robustness — outlier detection variants**, the seeded family member named first in ROADMAP §9 ("Upstream robustness family — active health checks, **outlier detection variants**, circuit breakers, retries + hedging, per-protocol connection pooling"). The within-(A) sub-pick (outlier detection vs circuit breakers expansion vs retries vs TCP-proxy pooling) settled on **outlier detection** because: (1) it is the natural next variant in the parent-charter list (active HC at 12 → connection pooling at 13 → outlier detection is the next-named primitive); (2) it **maximally reuses the 12.x + 13.x infrastructure**: the 12.1 `EndpointHealth` ejection seam at `pick()` + the 12.2 no-healthy-upstream synth-503 path + the 13.x configurable-status synthetic backend + the 13.1 `Driver::Http1KeepAlive` harness driver are all reused verbatim — no new harness primitive is required; (3) it **introduces the project's fourth periodic-background primitive** (the ejection sweeper) extending the 12.2 + 13.1 + 13.2 triad cleanly (identical `tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken` cancellation discipline); (4) the scope is **tractable** at ~1500-2100 LoC with a known SPLIT seam (foundation slice 14.1 = schema + state machine + LB integration + stats; observable-behavior slice 14.2 = response-receipt hooks + ejection sweeper + fixture + parent-14 close — the standing 12/13 cadence); (5) the **foundation pressure is LOW** (no new top-level Cargo dep; the new `outlier.rs` module is internal to `envoy-cluster`); (6) **no foundations grant required** (D-3.2 hand-rolled scratch mandate — outlier detection is on the from-scratch list); (7) it **defers the more architecturally fraught variants** (success-rate-based + failure-percentage-based ejection — sliding-window aggregates; `consecutive_local_origin_failure` — connect-level error-class accounting; `enforcing_*` knobs — runtime-fractional-percent enforcement) cleanly via `deny_unknown_fields`. The within-family alternatives (circuit breakers expansion — smaller scope but less family momentum; retries + hedging — larger scope, mid-request mutation; TCP-proxy connection pooling — closes the 13.2 D7.1 row's TCP carve-out but is a TCP-side feature not an upstream-robustness feature) all defer to follow-up phases.

**ADR-0028 closure NOT picked at THIS session.** Option (H) — extracting `envoy-http2::Client` into a new `envoy-http-client` crate to break the dep cycle blocking H1-listener × H2-cluster dispatch — is the documented closure path from ADR-0039 Consequences. It remains a future foundations-pivot phase (named owner: a follow-up phase, NOT phase 14). Phase-14 stays a feature phase.

**Five-dimension scoring** (light pass; the standing 11/12/13 brainstorm framework). All within the continued-Upstream-robustness-family pool:

No SPEC edit to the parent-14 SPEC (preserved verbatim; the sub-phase SPECs carve from it). No PLAN/PROGRESS/REVIEW at THIS commit (those land at the 14.1 state-2 PLAN-write next session and onward).
