**§6.2 empirical-verification findings (HEAVY; 9 items; against `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0`).** Performed at THIS commit by a single general-purpose subagent dispatched foreground per `superpowers:writing-plans` + `feedback_execution_style` (controller-authored §6.2 verification deferred to a subagent for context isolation — the verification produced 80+ exploratory tool calls over ~12 minutes; subagent returned a structured findings report). Methodology: Docker bridge networking (macOS Docker Desktop with host LAN IPv4 binding `192.168.50.111` since `host.docker.internal` IPv6 route was broken) with (a) the project's existing `tests/helpers/health-aware-http1-backend/` Rust helper run on host ports 18080/18081 (`--per-path /fail=500` for the 5xx surface; HC-healthy variant at 18081) and (b) `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0` containers running H1 HCM listeners routing through STATIC clusters pointing at the host LAN backends; admin `/stats?filter=outlier_detection` + `/stats/prometheus` + `/clusters` scraped + data-plane requests driven via Python `http.client.HTTPConnection` (single keep-alive conn for the discriminating-observable surface). Both H1 + H2 sibling Envoys booted to verify protocol-agnostic stat namespace at the cluster construct site.

| # | Item | Finding | Disposition |
|---|------|---------|-------------|
| 1 | **Exact `outlier_detection` config shape + defaults** | `consecutive_5xx`/`consecutive_gateway_failure` default 5/5 (parent SPEC projected 5/5 ✓); `interval` default 10s; `base_ejection_time` default 30s; `max_ejection_percent` default 10 (NOT 100 — the fixture-0022 setting `max_ejection_percent: 100` is necessary to avoid the default 10% cap blocking single-endpoint ejection — single endpoint at default cap = floor(1*10/100) = 0 ejectable hosts → overflow without ejection; CRITICAL OBSERVATION the SPEC's fixture config correctly anticipates). Envoy strictly REJECTS unknown fields under `outlier_detection`. Full v3 accepted field set probed via deliberate-failure-on-unknown: `consecutive_5xx`, `consecutive_gateway_failure`, `consecutive_local_origin_failure`, `interval`, `base_ejection_time`, `max_ejection_percent`, `enforcing_*` family (6 names), `success_rate_*` family (3 names), `failure_percentage_*` family (3 names), `max_ejection_time`, `max_ejection_time_jitter`, `split_external_local_origin_errors`. **`interval_jitter` IS NOT a v3 field name** (Envoy rejected it; the parent SPEC §4 listed it as deferred but it doesn't exist — strike from the deferred list at 14.1 D1 doc comment). | **MATCHES** parent SPEC projection on defaults. **Minor doc cleanup at 14.1 D1**: drop `interval_jitter` from the rejected-fields comment (it's not a real field). The phase-14 minimum-viable subset is correct (`consecutive_5xx` + `consecutive_gateway_failure` + `interval` + `base_ejection_time` + `max_ejection_percent`); everything else rejected by `deny_unknown_fields`. No ADR (the doc cleanup is a PLAN lock-in). |
| 2 | **`outlier_detection` stat namespace** | Envoy emits **21 names** under `cluster.<n>.outlier_detection.*`: 1 gauge (`ejections_active`) + 20 counters split as: `ejections_total` (legacy), `ejections_overflow`, `ejections_enforced_total`, `ejections_consecutive_5xx` (legacy alias of `ejections_enforced_consecutive_5xx` in v1.33), `ejections_success_rate` (legacy), `ejections_detected_consecutive_5xx`, `ejections_detected_consecutive_gateway_failure`, `ejections_detected_consecutive_local_origin_failure`, `ejections_detected_failure_percentage`, `ejections_detected_local_origin_failure_percentage`, `ejections_detected_local_origin_success_rate`, `ejections_detected_success_rate`, `ejections_enforced_consecutive_5xx`, `ejections_enforced_consecutive_gateway_failure`, `ejections_enforced_consecutive_local_origin_failure`, `ejections_enforced_failure_percentage`, `ejections_enforced_local_origin_failure_percentage`, `ejections_enforced_local_origin_success_rate`, `ejections_enforced_success_rate`. The per-detector counters split into `_detected_`/`_enforced_` pairs (the `_enforced_*` counts only when the threshold-crossing actually drove an ejection — i.e., when `enforcing_*` knob permitted enforcement + the max-ejection-percent cap permitted it). | **DIVERGES MATERIALLY** from parent SPEC projection (5 names). **ADR-0041 ratifies** the revised 7-name minimum-viable subset envoy-rust emits: `ejections_active` (gauge), `ejections_enforced_total`, `ejections_overflow`, `ejections_detected_consecutive_5xx`, `ejections_enforced_consecutive_5xx`, `ejections_detected_consecutive_gateway_failure`, `ejections_enforced_consecutive_gateway_failure`. The remaining 14 Envoy-side names are `allowlist_envoy_only` at fixture 0022 (deferred detectors + legacy aliases). |
| 3 | **Initial endpoint ejection state** | At Envoy boot with `outlier_detection` configured + single endpoint: `ejections_active: 0`, all counters at 0, `/clusters` admin shows endpoint with empty `health_flags::` (no flags set). Endpoints are implicitly never-ejected. No warmup window. A single 200 GET does not change any state. | **MATCHES** parent SPEC projection. 14.1 D3 locks: `EndpointEjection` initial state `ejected_at_ns: 0` + zero counters; no probationary state. 14.2 fixture-0022 settle logic: NO settle wait needed; the discriminating sequence (3 fails → 4th synth-503) can start at t=0. No ADR. |
| 4 | **`max_ejection_percent` enforcement semantics** | 2-endpoint cluster with `max_ejection_percent: 50`, both data-plane-failing with `consecutive_5xx: 3`: drove 20 consecutive `/fail` (round-robin ~10 per endpoint). Settle: `ejections_active: 1`, `ejections_enforced_total: 1`, `ejections_detected_consecutive_5xx: 6` (detector fired 6 times across 6 interval-ticks), `ejections_overflow: 5` (5 ticks where threshold detected on 2nd endpoint but cap blocked). Cap formula: `floor(N * percent / 100)` (`floor(2*50/100) = 1` ejectable). Overflow re-fires PER detection-tick when cap blocks enforcement, NOT one-shot per host. With `max_ejection_percent: 100`: overflow stays 0 (cap never hit). | **MATCHES** parent SPEC projection. 14.1 D3 locks the cap formula `floor(N * pct / 100)` at the ejection-decision site; the overflow counter increments per-detection-tick on cap-block. Fixture 0022's `max_ejection_percent: 100` keeps overflow at 0. No ADR. |
| 5 | **Ejection-time semantics** | With `base_ejection_time: 5s`, `interval: 1s`, `consecutive_5xx: 3`: triggered ejection at t=0; polled `ejections_active` every 0.5s. Observed un-eject at t ≈ 5.62-6.13s (one interval-tick beyond the 5s mark). The interval-driven loop re-evaluates each tick whether `now - eject_time >= base_ejection_time`. After un-ejection: `ejections_active: 0`, per-endpoint internal consecutive_5xx counter resets (a single fresh 200 succeeds; 3 new fails needed to re-eject). Sequential ejection→un-eject→re-eject sequences showed ejection-duration ≈ 5-6s regardless of N (the `* num_ejections` multiplier exists in Envoy docs but is NOT material at minimum-viable). | **MATCHES** parent SPEC projection. 14.2 D7 sweeper locks: each `interval` tick, re-eval each ejected endpoint; un-eject if `now_monotonic - eject_time >= base_ejection_time`. Per-endpoint counters reset on un-eject AND on any 2xx/3xx/4xx response (D4). The `* num_ejections` multiplier is out per parent §4. No ADR. |
| 6 | **Discriminating observable for fixture 0022** | Exact byte-level capture (4-request sequence over single keepalive conn, `consecutive_5xx: 3`, single endpoint serving 500 on `/fail`): request 1-3 = status 500 + len 13 + body `server error\n` + `x-envoy-upstream-service-time: 2/0/0`; request 4-5 = status 503 + len 19 + body `no healthy upstream` + `x-envoy-upstream-service-time` ABSENT. Synth-503 headers: `content-length: 19`, `content-type: text/plain`, `server: envoy`, NO upstream-service-time. The 19-byte body matches ADR-0037 projection exactly. Counter values after 4 requests: `ejections_active: 1`, `ejections_consecutive_5xx: 1`, `ejections_detected_consecutive_5xx: 1`, `ejections_enforced_consecutive_5xx: 1`, `ejections_enforced_total: 1`, `ejections_total: 1`, `ejections_consecutive_gateway_failure: 0` (backend 500 ≠ gateway-failure 502/503/504). | **MATCHES** parent SPEC projection completely. 14.2 D8.1 fixture-0022 bilateral assertions lock-in: request 1-3 status 500 + body `server error\n` + upstream-service-time PRESENT; request 4 status 503 + body `no healthy upstream` (19 bytes) + upstream-service-time ABSENT; post-settle counters `ejections_active == 1`, `ejections_enforced_total == 1`, `ejections_enforced_consecutive_5xx == 1`, `ejections_detected_consecutive_5xx == 1`, `ejections_overflow == 0`. No ADR. |
| 7 | **Composition with active HC** | 2-endpoint cluster with HC + OD both configured (18080 HC-bad+data-bad; 18081 HC-good+data-bad): both `cluster.<n>.health_check.*` AND `outlier_detection.*` stat trees emit simultaneously. 18080 (HC-failing): `health_check.failure` ticks; `/clusters` admin shows `health_flags::/failed_active_hc`; excluded from `pick()` so receives no data-plane traffic → its OD counters stay at 0. 18081 (HC-ok, data-bad): after 3 `/fail` via `pick()`, OD ejects it; `/clusters` shows `health_flags::/failed_outlier_check`; `membership_healthy: 0/2`; 4th request returns synth-503. HC continues probing OD-ejected endpoints (the two systems operate independently; AND-composition computed at `pick()` time via union of health-flag bits). | **MATCHES** parent SPEC projection. 14.1 D5 locks the AND-composition at `pick()`: candidate iff `EndpointHealth::is_healthy() AND !EndpointEjection::is_ejected()`; both signals are independent bits; HC continues probing OD-ejected endpoints (no coupling). No ADR. |
| 8 | **H1 vs H2 sibling consistency** | H2 upstream cluster (`typed_extension_protocol_options...http2_protocol_options: {}`) + same OD config. H1-only helper backend doesn't speak H2c → connect-failure → Envoy emits synth-503 `upstream connect error`. After 3 such requests: identical `cluster.<n>.outlier_detection.*` stat namespace emitted (same 21 names); ejection-on-failure semantics identical (`ejections_active: 1`, `ejections_enforced_consecutive_5xx: 1`); subsequent requests get synth-503 `no healthy upstream` (19 bytes). True H2 200/500 mix not tested (out of time budget; needs H2-speaking backend) but stat-namespace parity + ejection-on-failure parity confirmed. OD logic is protocol-agnostic in Envoy. | **MATCHES** parent SPEC projection. The state-machine + sweeper + stat namespace are protocol-agnostic; the response-receipt hook surface differs (H1 router vs H2 HCM post-dispatch) but both call the same `Cluster::record_response`. 14.2 D4 wires both. The split-seam decision picked foundation-cohesion (NOT protocol-cohesion as 13.x picked) explicitly because of this protocol-agnosticity. No ADR. |
| 9 | **Synth-status bypass + connect-failure classification** | `consecutive_5xx: 1`, `consecutive_gateway_failure: 1`, endpoint = `127.0.0.1:1` (unreachable). Request 1: status 503 + len 167 + body `upstream connect error or disconnect/reset before headers...`. After this single request: `ejections_active: 1`, `ejections_detected_consecutive_5xx: 1` AND `ejections_detected_consecutive_gateway_failure: 1` (BOTH detectors fire — connect-fail classified as BOTH 5xx-equivalent AND gateway-failure for the picked endpoint), `ejections_enforced_consecutive_5xx: 1`, `ejections_enforced_total: 1`. Request 2: status 503 + len 19 + body `no healthy upstream` — all counters UNCHANGED (the synth-503 from `pick() -> None` does NOT feed back; no endpoint to attribute). | **DIVERGES MATERIALLY** from parent SPEC §5.7 uniform-bypass projection. **ADR-0041 ratifies** the nuanced classification: connect-failure synth paths (picked endpoint failed to connect) DO call `Cluster::record_response` with status 503 classified as BOTH `consecutive_5xx` AND `consecutive_gateway_failure`; `pick() -> None` no-healthy-upstream synth-503 path does NOT call `record_response` (no endpoint to attribute). 14.2 D4 wires the bypass at the router-arm dispatch site explicitly. |

**§6.2 synthesis: 7 of 9 items MATCHED the parent SPEC's projections; 2 items DIVERGED MATERIALLY** — Item 2 stat-namespace expansion + Item 9 connect-failure classification + synth-status bypass nuance. **Both divergences ratified by ADR-0041** landing inline at THIS commit (mirrors ADR-0034 / ADR-0037 §6.2 empirical-revision precedents). The §6.2 conditional ADR-0041 slot (parent SPEC §7 option B; "Numbered `ADR-0041` if the split ADR took `ADR-0040`. **Recommended posture: verify all 9; land the revision ADR if any differ.**") FIRES as projected.

**Split decision lock-in (per `feedback_pick_recommendation` + parent SPEC §6.1):**
- **Seam choice:** foundation-cohesion (14.1 schema + state machine + LB integration + stats foundation slice → 14.2 response-receipt hooks + sweeper + fixture + parent close). The parent SPEC §6.1 recommended posture. Alternative protocol-cohesion seam (H1-side + H2-side; phase-13's choice) was rejected because the §6.2 item-8 finding showed outlier-detection is PROTOCOL-AGNOSTIC at the state-machine + sweeper + stat-namespace layers (only the response-receipt hook surface differs between H1 router vs H2 HCM post-dispatch). Splitting by protocol would duplicate the state machine + sweeper across sub-phases and fragment the contract surface. The foundation-cohesion split keeps the state machine + sweeper atomic in 14.1's foundation + 14.2's observable-behavior wire-up.
- **14.1 scope:** D1 envoy-config `OutlierDetection` schema + D2 validator + D3 `EndpointEjection` state machine + D5 `Cluster::pick()` AND-composition + D6 outlier-detection stats foundation slice (the 7-name minimum-viable subset per ADR-0041) + optional D8.2 fuzz seed `cluster_outlier_detection.yaml` (corpus 21 → 22; recommended at 14.1). NO new fixture; acceptance = regression-equivalence on the 21 existing Docker-gated fixtures (the 05.1 / 07.1 / 12.1 inert-foundation pattern; `outlier_detection.is_none()` short-circuit at `pick()` keeps the existing 21 fixtures unchanged). ~700-900 LoC. The 14.1 PLAN-writer locks the 7-name stat-namespace + the connect-failure classifier semantics + the §6.2 item-1 doc cleanup (drop `interval_jitter` from rejected-fields comment).
- **14.2 scope:** D4 response-receipt hooks (H1 router `crates/envoy-http1/src/router.rs::write_proxied_response` + H2 sibling `crates/envoy-http2/src/hcm.rs` post-dispatch site; the connect-failure synth path also calls `record_response` per ADR-0041 §6.2 item-9; the `pick() -> None` no-healthy-upstream synth-503 path does NOT call `record_response`) + D7 `OutlierEjectionSweeper` + `OutlierManager` external sibling registry (the **FOURTH periodic-background primitive** after 12.2 + 13.1 + 13.2; identical `tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken` cancellation discipline + `pub async fn shutdown(self)`) + D8.1 fixture 0022-upstream-outlier-detection-consecutive-5xx (reuses 13.x `health-aware-http1-backend` + 13.1 `Driver::Http1KeepAlive` verbatim; bilateral assertions per ADR-0041 §6.2 item-6) + D8.2 fuzz seed (if not landed at 14.1) + D8.3 in-process backstop (BOTH eject + un-eject convergence directions per parent §6.4) + parent-14 close (flips ROADMAP rows `14.2` AND parent-14 `14` `in-progress → done` SIMULTANEOUSLY per the closing-sub-phase invariant; mirrors the 02.2/03.2/05.4/07.2/08.2/12.2/13.2 precedent). ~900-1200 LoC.
- **Cycle-resolution decision (carried to both sub-phases):** the `EndpointEjection` state machine lives in `envoy-cluster` next to 12.1 `EndpointHealth`; `Cluster::record_response` method is declared on `Cluster` (no new trait); `OutlierEjectionSweeper` + `OutlierManager` live in a new module `crates/envoy-cluster/src/outlier.rs` (~230 LoC; mirrors 13.1's `envoy-http1::pool` + 13.2's `envoy-http2::pool` placement-inside-existing-crate pattern). `OutlierManager` is an external sibling registry to `ClusterManager` wired at `envoy-bin` startup (mirrors `H1PoolManager`/`H2PoolManager`/`envoy-health::Scheduler` verbatim). NO new top-level Cargo dep; NO new workspace member; NO cycle. Recommended posture: NO ADR (the placement is ordinary structure mirroring the established 12.2 + 13.x patterns). The 14.1/14.2 PLAN-writers verify the seam shape at PLAN-time.
- **Synth-status bypass nuance (§6.2 item-9 lock-in per ADR-0041):** the parent SPEC §5.7's uniform-bypass projection is NUANCED to apply ONLY to the `pick() -> None` no-healthy-upstream synth-503 (no endpoint to attribute). Connect-failure synth-503/synth-502 paths DO call `Cluster::record_response` (picked endpoint attributed as 5xx + gateway-failure). 14.2 D4 wires the bypass at the router-arm dispatch site EXPLICITLY (the no-healthy-upstream code-path short-circuits; the connect-failure code-path calls).
- **TCP / H3 carve-outs (parent §4):** TCP-side outlier detection (connect failures on TCP-proxy clusters) defers with the TCP pool follow-up; H3 defers with the HTTP/3 family. Phase-14 is HTTP-only at minimum-viable scope.

ADR-0040 mirrors ADR-0036 / ADR-0038 narrative shape (split with 5 options considered + rationale). ADR-0041 mirrors ADR-0034 / ADR-0037 narrative shape (§6.2 empirical-revision with revised projection + sub-phase SPEC lock-ins). The conditional ADR-cycle-resolution slot (parent SPEC §7 option C; new-module + external `OutlierManager`-registry) does NOT fire — placement is ordinary structure. The conditional ADR-foundations-grant slot (parent SPEC §7 option D) does NOT fire — outlier detection is hand-rolled atop existing pulled crates per D-3.2. The conditional ADR-H2-no-healthy-upstream-reconciliation slot (parent SPEC §7 option E) does NOT fire — the §6.2 item-8 verification confirmed H2 parity on the 19-byte body. **Next available ADR number after this commit: ADR-0042.**

### Phase-14.2 state-2 PLAN-write

This subsection records the phase-14.2 state-2 standalone PLAN-write (THIS commit) — the CLOSING-sub-phase PLAN-write of the parent-14 outlier-detection phase, mirroring the 14.1 `9a56e85` (freshest standalone-PLAN-write) + 13.2 `8c7d8a23` + 12.2 `6a3b3329` (closest CLOSING-sub-phase PLAN-write) precedents verbatim. Prior 4-top-pointer blocks demoted to `_Historical_` per D-3.5 + D-3.4.

**§6.1 split-gate verdict: NO SPLIT, NO NEST-SPLIT.** The materialized PLAN is **11 tasks; ~1100–1300 LoC** (production ~430, tests/backstop ~520, fixture/driver/docs ~330), comfortably under the `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §6.1 ~1500-LoC / ~25-task gate. The small overage vs the SPEC §6.1 ~900–1200 projection is the M4 serialization (~70 LoC) + the keep-alive driver extension (~90 LoC), both PLAN-time SPEC corrections. The parent-14 split (ADR-0040) already absorbed the over-gate scope into 14.1 + 14.2; 14.2 does NOT nest-split (standalone-PLAN posture per `feedback_pick_recommendation`).

**PLAN structure (11 tasks).** Task 1 — `ejection.rs` + `cluster.rs` extension: the M4 per-endpoint serialization lock (`ejected_at: std::sync::Mutex<Option<Instant>>`, held by the `Cluster::record_response` compound + the sweeper's un-eject; doubles as the eject-timestamp) + the M5 tie-test strengthening (expose `EndpointEjectionStats` from the helper; assert `enforced_consecutive_5xx == 1 && enforced_consecutive_gateway_failure == 0`; drop the vestigial `let _ = stats_active;`) + the M6 `max_ejection_percent = 0` edge test + cap-site comment. Task 2 — D4 H1 hook (a single `cluster.record_response(endpoint, outgoing.status)` after the `match stream_or_synth` block in `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs` ~`:681`, inside the `if let Some(endpoint)` block; the no-healthy `else` arm does NOT record) + the 13.2 A-M2 stale `tokio::sync::Mutex` → `parking_lot::Mutex` comment fix at `pool.rs:322`. Task 3 — D4 H2 hook (success-arm `record_response(endpoint, upstream_resp.status)` after the `:424-427` increments + connect-failure-arm `record_response(endpoint, 502)` before the `return finalize_h2_stream` in the `Err(e)` arm `:399-417`; the `pick() -> None` arm does NOT record). Task 4 — D7 `OutlierEjectionSweeper` + `OutlierManager` in a new `crates/envoy-cluster/src/outlier.rs` (the FOURTH periodic-background primitive; `tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken` + `pub async fn shutdown(self)`; `OutlierDetectionState` gains `base_ejection_time` + `interval` Durations populated in `from_bootstrap`). Task 5 — `envoy-bin` startup wiring + drain shutdown (mirrors the health scheduler). Task 6 — extend `Driver::Http1KeepAlive` with 3 `#[serde(default)]` optional fields (`expected_body` / `require_header_present` / `require_header_absent`) + a `read_h1_response_full` helper (SPEC correction B-3). Task 7 — fixture `0022` (3 YAMLs + README) + Docker-gated wrapper `tests/differential/tests/upstream_outlier_detection.rs`. Task 8 — in-process backstop `crates/envoy-bin/tests/upstream_outlier_detection.rs` (eject + un-eject [short 5s `base_ejection_time`] + synth-503 5-header presence). Task 9 — D9 docs (BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT non-amendment) + M8 `allowlist_envoy_only` count reconciliation (empirical at the state-3 Docker run). Task 10 — state-4 phase-done verification. Task 11 — state-6 CLOSING-sub-phase close-out (flips rows `14.2` AND parent `14` `in-progress → done`; `[parent 14 done]`; a later session, after the state-5 REVIEW.md is approved).

**6 PLAN-time SPEC corrections (B-1…B-6)** logged in PROGRESS Task 1 preamble (the 06.2 → 14.1 cadence): **B-1** the H1 response-receipt site is `crates/envoy-http1/src/router.rs::construct_proxied_response` (`:87-168`; increments at `:95`/`:97`), NOT `write_proxied_response`, and `endpoint` is NOT in scope inside it — so the D4-H1 hook lands at the CALLER in `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs` after the dispatch `match` (~`:681`), NOT in `router.rs`; **B-2** the H1 no-healthy synth-503 short-circuit is at `hcm.rs:691` (`synth_no_healthy_upstream`, builder `:1057-1077`), NOT `:582`; **B-3** `Driver::Http1KeepAlive` is reused-plus-EXTENDED (the HEAD driver reads status only; `Http1KeepAliveRequest` `:314-319` carries no body/header fields — Task 6 adds them backward-compatibly); **B-4** the §5.1 sweeper-field sketch is reconciled to on-disk types (no runtime `OutlierDetectionConfig`; the two `Duration`s live on `OutlierDetectionState`; the sweeper is `{ cancel, join }`); **B-5** H1 connect-failure synths `502` / pool-overflow `503`, H2 no-healthy synths `502` (`synth_h2_502`, `:240-257`) not 503 (pre-existing, differential-inert for the H1-only fixture; D4 records neither no-healthy arm); **B-6** backend `per_class_body(500)` is `b"server error\n"` = 13 bytes (`tests/helpers/health-aware-http1-backend/src/main.rs:121-133`), `200` on `/` empty.

**§6.2 lock-ins pulled forward** (ADR-0041; NOT re-verified): item-5 (un-eject at next tick after `now - eject_time >= base_ejection_time`; counters reset on un-eject AND on 2xx/3xx/4xx), item-6 (fixture bilateral observable — 500×3 + `server error\n` (13B) + `x-envoy-upstream-service-time` PRESENT; 503 + `no healthy upstream` (19B) + that header ABSENT; counters exact), item-8 (H1/H2 share the namespace; H2 hook at the HCM post-dispatch site), item-9 (the `pick() -> None` synth-503 does NOT record; the connect-failure synth path DOES record — 502/503 ticks both detectors automatically).

**M4 discharge (the most substantive 14.1 carryforward; the 12.1-M2 analog).** At 14.2 the `EndpointEjection` writers become genuinely concurrent — one per in-flight request via the D4 hook + the D7 sweeper — violating the 14.1 `Relaxed` single-writer premise. Resolution (Task 1, PLAN §0.A #4): the per-endpoint `ejected_at` `Mutex` serializes the `Cluster::record_response` compound (record → cap-check → eject + stamp) and the sweeper's per-endpoint un-eject ⇒ exactly one serialized writer per endpoint; `pick()`'s read side stays lock-free (`is_ejected()` is a single `Relaxed` `AtomicBool` load). The `Mutex<Option<Instant>>` payload doubles as the eject-timestamp the sweeper reads for `base_ejection_time` (14.1's `EndpointEjection` carried no timestamp). The 14.2 REVIEW (named M4 owner) verifies this discharge.
